 		OutOrigin = Quad->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(MeshBounds.Origin);
 		OutExtent = Quad->GetComponentTransform().TransformVector(MeshBounds.BoxExtent);
 	}
 }
+
+FString UGBPixelVfxComponent::DescribeQuadForDiag() const
+{
+	FString Desc;
+	Desc += FString::Printf(TEXT("quad=%d reg=%d vis=%d parent=%s mesh=%s mat=%s "),
+		Quad ? 1 : 0,
+		Quad && Quad->IsRegistered() ? 1 : 0,
+		Quad && Quad->IsVisible() ? 1 : 0,
+		Quad && Quad->GetAttachParent() ? *Quad->GetAttachParent()->GetName() : TEXT("none"),
+		Quad && Quad->GetStaticMesh() ? *Quad->GetStaticMesh()->GetName() : TEXT("none"),
+		QuadMaterial ? *QuadMaterial->GetName() : TEXT("none"));
+	if (QuadMaterial)
+	{
+		float U = -1.f;
+		float V = -1.f;
+		float W = -1.f;
+		float H = -1.f;
+		QuadMaterial->GetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameU"), U);
+		QuadMaterial->GetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameV"), V);
+		QuadMaterial->GetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameW"), W);
+		QuadMaterial->GetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameH"), H);
+		UTexture* Atlas = nullptr;
+		QuadMaterial->GetTextureParameterValue(TEXT("Atlas"), Atlas);
+		Desc += FString::Printf(TEXT("frame=%.2f,%.2f,%.2f,%.2f atlas=%s "),
+			U, V, W, H, Atlas ? *Atlas->GetName() : TEXT("none"));
+	}
+	Desc += FString::Printf(TEXT("light=%d lightreg=%d intensity=%.1f radius=%.0f"),
+		ClipLight ? 1 : 0,
+		ClipLight && ClipLight->IsRegistered() ? 1 : 0,
+		ClipLight ? ClipLight->Intensity : 0.f,
+		ClipLight ? ClipLight->AttenuationRadius : 0.f);
+	return Desc;
+}

+			{
+				const FString PngPath = FPaths::ProjectContentDir() / TEXT("Data/Vfx/FluidFire.png");
+				TArray<uint8> PngBytes;
+				uint64 ZeroBytes = 0;
+				uint64 NonZeroAlpha = 0;
+				uint8 MinByte = 255;
+				uint8 MaxByte = 0;
+				if (FFileHelper::LoadFileToArray(PngBytes, *PngPath))
+				{
+					FImage ProbeImage;
+					if (FImageUtils::DecompressImage(PngBytes.GetData(), PngBytes.Num(), ProbeImage))
+					{
+						for (uint8 Byte : ProbeImage.RawData)
+						{
+							MinByte = FMath::Min(MinByte, Byte);
+							MaxByte = FMath::Max(MaxByte, Byte);
+							if (Byte == 0)
+							{
+								++ZeroBytes;
+							}
+						}
+						const int64 PxCount = ProbeImage.RawData.Num() / 4;
+						for (int64 p = 0; p < PxCount; ++p)
+						{
+							if (ProbeImage.RawData[p * 4 + 3] != 0)
+							{
+								++NonZeroAlpha;
+							}
+						}
+						TArray<FColor> Dump;
+						Dump.SetNum(PxCount);
+						FMemory::Memcpy(Dump.GetData(), ProbeImage.RawData.GetData(), ProbeImage.RawData.Num());
+						TArray64<uint8> Compressed;
+						FImageUtils::PNGCompressImageArray(ProbeImage.SizeX, ProbeImage.SizeY,
+							TArrayView64<const FColor>(Dump.GetData(), Dump.Num()), Compressed);
+						TArray<uint8> Bytes;
+						Bytes.Append(Compressed.GetData(), Compressed.Num());
+						FFileHelper::SaveArrayToFile(Bytes, *(FPaths::ProjectSavedDir() / TEXT("PixelVfx/atlas_dump.png")));
+					}
+				}
+				UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX showcase diag atlasdump bytes=%d min=%d max=%d zero=%llu opaque=%llu fmt=%d %dx%d"),
+					PngBytes.Num(), MinByte, MaxByte, ZeroBytes, NonZeroAlpha,
+					(int32)ProbeImage.Format, ProbeImage.SizeX, ProbeImage.SizeY);
+			}
 			UStaticMesh* PlaneMesh = Cast<UStaticMesh>(StaticLoadObject(UStaticMesh::StaticClass(), nullptr,
 				TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Plane.Plane")));
